     1	#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
     2	# Life on air (P3), revised analyses after review round 1 (12 October 2026). Supersedes h123_analysis.R,
     3	# which is kept for provenance (outputs in results/h123/).
     4	#
     5	# Corrections (finding IDs from lifeonair/reviews/round1/findings.tsv):
     6	#   R1-01  corrected CoxL form calls (fix_coxL_v2.py; guild_genome_equivalents_v2.tsv)
     7	#   R1-02  group 2a [NiFe] reported separately, not part of the hydrogenase control
     8	#   R1-19  reads to proteins of two refinement-dropped _sub representatives excluded
     9	#   R1-06  canonical transect only (sites 1-60); 62 and 64 are special-interest locations
    10	#   R1-03  pure ERA5 1995-2025 (fetch_era5_2025.py), so antecedent rain covers expedition 5
    11	#   R1-11  temporal test as an explicit within/between-site decomposition with an equivalence bound
    12	#   R1-12  site-visit random effects wherever samples share a visit
    13	#   R1-13  residual spatial autocorrelation and block-scale sensitivity
    14	#   R1-15/16 PERMANOVA with restricted permutations; synchrony within position
    15	#   R1-18  Benjamini-Hochberg within declared families of secondary tests
    16	#   R1-21  pseudocount sensitivity for paired contrasts
    17	#   R1-31  marginal R2 (Nakagawa) instead of the ad hoc variance share
    18	# New analyses (round-1 consultation, lifeonair/reviews/round1_consult/):
    19	#   A1  scaling of each marker with DNA yield (biomass proxy)
    20	#   A2  lineage turnover vs within-genus carrier fraction (root contrast and aridity)
    21	#   A3  root vs surface filters across markers; surface contrast vs temperature and soil moisture
    22	#   A4  carbon-fixation (CBB) co-carriage by hydrogenase group
    23	#   A6  carrier representation vs copies per carrier
    24	#   A7  geochemistry (XRF) as an alternative to aridity
    25	#
    26	# GE (genome equivalents) are relative capacity: marker copies per ribosomal-protein genome equivalent.
    27	#
    28	#   micromamba run -n eq-lifeonair Rscript lifeonair/code/h123_v2.R   (repository root)
    29	
    30	suppressMessages({ library(lme4); library(vegan); library(ape); library(permute) })
    31	set.seed(20261012)
    32	IN  <- "lifeonair/results/inputs"; RD <- "lifeonair/results/reads"
    33	OUT <- "lifeonair/results/h123_v2"; dir.create(OUT, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)
    34	rd  <- function(f, ...) read.delim(f, sep = "\t", quote = "", comment.char = "", stringsAsFactors = FALSE,
    35	                                   check.names = FALSE, ...)
    36	wr  <- function(x, f) write.table(x, file.path(OUT, f), sep = "\t", quote = FALSE, row.names = FALSE)
    37	EPS <- 0.01; NB <- 2000
    38	FOCAL    <- c("hydB_1l", "hydB_1h", "hydB_2a", "coxL_formI")
    39	CONTROLS <- c("hydB_other_nife", "coxL_other")
    40	MARKERS  <- c(FOCAL, CONTROLS)
    41	ORPHANS  <- readLines(file.path(RD, "orphan_sub_reps.txt"))
    42	
    43	meta <- rd(file.path(IN, "samples.tsv"))
    44	meta <- meta[meta$trip != "" & meta$compartment != "" & meta$site %in% 1:60, ]
    45	site_env <- rd(file.path(IN, "env_site.tsv"))
    46	site_env <- merge(site_env, rd(file.path(IN, "nasa_power_precip_climatology.tsv"))[, c("site", "map_power_mm")], by = "site")
    47	st_env <- rd(file.path(IN, "env_site_trip.tsv"))
    48	tmp <- rd(file.path(IN, "per_trip_site_temperature.tsv")); tmp$trip <- paste0("trip", tmp$trip)
    49	st_env <- merge(st_env, tmp, by = c("site", "trip"), all.x = TRUE)
    50	
    51	load_ge <- function(file = "guild_genome_equivalents_v2.tsv", floor = 60, drop_trips = character()) {
    52	  g <- rd(file.path(RD, file)); g <- g[g$id_floor == floor & g$scg_genes_detected >= 10, ]
    53	  d <- merge(meta, g, by.x = "sample_id", by.y = "sample")
    54	  d[!d$trip %in% drop_trips, ]
    55	}
    56	d0 <- load_ge()
    57	d0$visit <- paste(d0$site, d0$trip, sep = "|")
    58	
    59	# DNA yield (extract concentration), biomass proxy; conflicting duplicate records and non-positive values dropped
    60	led <- rd(file.path(IN, "sample_ledger_dna.tsv"))
    61	led$dna <- suppressWarnings(as.numeric(led$dna_concentration))
    62	nconc <- tapply(led$dna, led$sample_id, function(x) length(unique(round(x[is.finite(x)], 6))))
    63	led <- led[led$sample_id %in% names(nconc)[nconc == 1] & is.finite(led$dna) & led$dna > 0, ]
    64	led <- led[!duplicated(led$sample_id), c("sample_id", "dna", "dna_kit")]
    65	d0 <- merge(d0, led, by = "sample_id", all.x = TRUE)
    66	
    67	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
    68	cells <- function(d, a, b, vars, eps = EPS) {          # visit-level log2 ratio of replicate medians
    69	  key <- paste(d$site, d$trip, sep = "|"); out <- NULL
    70	  for (k in unique(key)) {
    71	    x <- d[key == k & d$compartment == a, ]; y <- d[key == k & d$compartment == b, ]
    72	    if (!nrow(x) || !nrow(y)) next
    73	    row <- data.frame(site = x$site[1], trip = x$trip[1])
    74	    for (v in vars) row[[v]] <- log2((median(x[[v]]) + eps) / (median(y[[v]]) + eps))
    75	    out <- rbind(out, row)
    76	  }
    77	  out
    78	}
    79	add_excess <- function(c1) {
    80	  ctl <- rowMeans(c1[, CONTROLS])
    81	  for (v in FOCAL) c1[[paste0("excess_", v)]] <- c1[[v]] - ctl
    82	  c1
    83	}
    84	site_boot <- function(df, f) {           # cluster bootstrap over sites (row indices, fast)
    85	  idx <- split(seq_len(nrow(df)), df$site); s <- names(idx)
    86	  replicate(NB, f(df[unlist(idx[sample(s, replace = TRUE)], use.names = FALSE), , drop = FALSE]))
    87	}
    88	q95 <- function(x) quantile(x, c(.025, .975), na.rm = TRUE)
    89	site_wilcox <- function(df, v) { s <- tapply(df[[v]], df$site, mean); wilcox.test(s, exact = FALSE)$p.value }
    90	contrast_table <- function(cc, vars, label) do.call(rbind, lapply(vars, function(v) {
    91	  b <- q95(site_boot(cc, function(x) median(x[[v]])))
    92	  data.frame(contrast = label, quantity = v, n_visits = nrow(cc), n_sites = length(unique(cc$site)),
    93	             median_log2 = median(cc[[v]]), ci_lo = b[1], ci_hi = b[2], frac_below0 = mean(cc[[v]] < 0),
    94	             p_site_wilcoxon = site_wilcox(cc, v), row.names = NULL)
    95	}))
    96	r2m <- function(f) {    # Nakagawa marginal R2 for a Gaussian lmer
    97	  vf <- var(as.vector(model.matrix(f) %*% fixef(f))); vr <- sum(as.data.frame(VarCorr(f))$vcov)
    98	  vf / (vf + vr)
    99	}
   100	
   101	# ================================================================== H1 root contrast (relative representation)
   102	c1 <- add_excess(cells(d0, "PlantRoot", "Deep", MARKERS))
   103	wr(c1, "h1_visit_log2fc_root_vs_bulk.tsv")
   104	h1 <- contrast_table(c1, c(MARKERS, paste0("excess_", FOCAL)), "root/bulk")
   105	cs <- add_excess(cells(d0, "Surface", "Deep", MARKERS))
   106	wr(cs, "h1_visit_log2fc_surface_vs_bulk.tsv")
   107	hs <- contrast_table(cs, c(MARKERS, paste0("excess_", FOCAL)), "surface/bulk")
   108	crs <- add_excess(cells(d0, "PlantRoot", "Surface", MARKERS))
   109	hrs <- contrast_table(crs, c(MARKERS, paste0("excess_", FOCAL)), "root/surface")
   110	contr <- rbind(h1, hs, hrs)
   111	contr$q_BH_within_contrast <- ave(contr$p_site_wilcoxon, contr$contrast, FUN = function(p) p.adjust(p, "BH"))
   112	wr(contr, "contrasts_by_position.tsv")
   113	
   114	# pseudocount sensitivity (no paired visit median is zero for 1l; ratios are finite without a pseudocount)
   115	pc_sens <- do.call(rbind, lapply(c(0, 0.001, 0.01, 0.1), function(e) {
   116	  x <- add_excess(cells(d0, "PlantRoot", "Deep", MARKERS, eps = e))
   117	  fm <- function(v) { z <- x[[v]][is.finite(x[[v]])]; median(z) }
   118	  data.frame(pseudocount = e, n_visits = nrow(x), n_finite_1l = sum(is.finite(x$hydB_1l)),
   119	             n_finite_excess_1l = sum(is.finite(x$excess_hydB_1l)), root_1l = fm("hydB_1l"),
   120	             root_excess_1l = fm("excess_hydB_1l"), root_1h = fm("hydB_1h"), root_2a = fm("hydB_2a"),
   121	             root_formI = fm("coxL_formI"))
   122	}))
   123	wr(pc_sens, "h1_pseudocount_sensitivity.tsv")
   124	
   125	h1_trip <- do.call(rbind, lapply(split(c1, c1$trip), function(x)
   126	  data.frame(trip = x$trip[1], n_visits = nrow(x), median_1l = median(x$hydB_1l), median_excess_1l = median(x$excess_hydB_1l))))
   127	wr(h1_trip, "h1_by_trip.tsv")
   128	
   129	# marker x position joint model (root and bulk; all six markers; sample effect for within-sample correlation)
   130	long <- function(d, comps) do.call(rbind, lapply(MARKERS, function(v) {
   131	  x <- d[d$compartment %in% comps, c("sample_id", "site", "trip", "visit", "compartment")]
   132	  x$marker <- v; x$y <- log2(d[[v]][d$compartment %in% comps] + EPS); x }))
   133	joint <- function(comps, ref_comp = "Deep") {
   134	  dl <- long(d0, comps)
   135	  dl$marker <- relevel(factor(dl$marker), ref = "hydB_other_nife")
   136	  dl$compartment <- relevel(factor(dl$compartment), ref = ref_comp)
   137	  f1 <- lmer(y ~ marker * compartment + trip + (1 | site) + (1 | visit) + (1 | sample_id), data = dl, REML = FALSE)
   138	  f0 <- lmer(y ~ marker + compartment + trip + (1 | site) + (1 | visit) + (1 | sample_id), data = dl, REML = FALSE)
   139	  a <- anova(f0, f1); fx <- summary(f1)$coefficients; k <- grepl(":", rownames(fx))
   140	  data.frame(positions = paste(comps, collapse = "+"), term = rownames(fx)[k], fx[k, 1:2, drop = FALSE],
   141	             LRT_chisq = a$Chisq[2], LRT_df = a$Df[2], LRT_p = a$`Pr(>Chisq)`[2], row.names = NULL)
   142	}
   143	jm <- rbind(joint(c("PlantRoot", "Deep")), joint(c("Surface", "Deep")))
   144	wr(jm, "joint_marker_by_position_models.tsv")
   145	
   146	# per-marker position effect: marginal R2 and LRT, with visit random effect (R1-12, R1-31)
   147	pos_eff <- do.call(rbind, lapply(MARKERS, function(v) {
   148	  x <- d0; x$y <- log2(x[[v]] + EPS)
   149	  f1 <- lmer(y ~ compartment + trip + (1 | site) + (1 | visit), data = x, REML = FALSE)
   150	  f0 <- lmer(y ~ trip + (1 | site) + (1 | visit), data = x, REML = FALSE)
   151	  data.frame(marker = v, position_LRT_p = anova(f0, f1)$`Pr(>Chisq)`[2],
   152	             marginal_R2_full = r2m(f1), marginal_R2_trip_only = r2m(f0), delta_marginal_R2_position = r2m(f1) - r2m(f0))
   153	}))
   154	wr(pos_eff, "position_effect_by_marker.tsv")
   155	
   156	# ================================================================== A1 scaling with DNA yield
   157	dd <- d0[is.finite(d0$dna), ]
   158	dd$ldna <- log2(dd$dna)
   159	dd$ldna_visit <- ave(dd$ldna, dd$visit); dd$ldna_within <- dd$ldna - dd$ldna_visit
   160	kit_ok <- names(which(tapply(dd$dna_kit, dd$visit, function(k) length(unique(k)) == 1)))
   161	a1 <- NULL
   162	for (v in c(MARKERS, "r_1l_1h", "r_1l_2a", "r_1l_formI")) {
   163	  y <- switch(v, r_1l_1h = log2(dd$hydB_1l / dd$hydB_1h), r_1l_2a = log2(dd$hydB_1l / dd$hydB_2a),
   164	              r_1l_formI = log2(dd$hydB_1l / dd$coxL_formI), log2(dd[[v]] + EPS))
   165	  x <- dd; x$y <- y; x <- x[is.finite(x$y), ]
   166	  f <- lmer(y ~ ldna + compartment + trip + dna_kit + (1 | site) + (1 | visit), data = x, REML = FALSE)
   167	  g <- lmer(y ~ ldna_within + ldna_visit + compartment + trip + dna_kit + (1 | site) + (1 | visit), data = x, REML = FALSE)
   168	  xs <- x[x$visit %in% kit_ok, ]
   169	  h <- lmer(y ~ ldna + compartment + trip + (1 | site) + (1 | visit), data = xs, REML = FALSE)
   170	  xb <- x[x$compartment == "Deep", ]
   171	  b <- lmer(y ~ ldna + trip + dna_kit + (1 | site), data = xb, REML = FALSE)
   172	  cf <- function(m, t) summary(m)$coefficients[t, 1:2]
   173	  a1 <- rbind(a1, data.frame(response = v, n = nrow(x), slope_ldna = cf(f, "ldna")[1], se = cf(f, "ldna")[2],
   174	    slope_within_visit = cf(g, "ldna_within")[1], se_within = cf(g, "ldna_within")[2],
   175	    slope_between_visit = cf(g, "ldna_visit")[1], se_between = cf(g, "ldna_visit")[2],
   176	    slope_same_kit_visits = cf(h, "ldna")[1], se_same_kit = cf(h, "ldna")[2], n_same_kit = nrow(xs),
   177	    slope_bulk_only = cf(b, "ldna")[1], se_bulk_only = cf(b, "ldna")[2],
   178	    # errors-in-variables: classical error with reliability lambda attenuates slope by lambda
   179	    slope_eiv_rel0.8 = cf(f, "ldna")[1] / 0.8, slope_eiv_rel0.6 = cf(f, "ldna")[1] / 0.6))
   180	}
   181	a1$implied_alpha <- 1 + a1$slope_ldna        # carriers ~ biomass^alpha (for single markers)
   182	wr(a1, "A1_dna_scaling.tsv")
   183	# root effect on 1l before and after DNA adjustment, same complete-case cohort (root + bulk)
   184	rb <- dd[dd$compartment %in% c("PlantRoot", "Deep"), ]; rb$y <- log2(rb$hydB_1l + EPS)
   185	m0 <- lmer(y ~ compartment + trip + dna_kit + (1 | site) + (1 | visit), data = rb, REML = FALSE)
   186	m1 <- lmer(y ~ compartment + ldna + trip + dna_kit + (1 | site) + (1 | visit), data = rb, REML = FALSE)
   187	a1b <- data.frame(model = c("root_effect_unadjusted", "root_effect_DNA_adjusted"),
   188	                  n = nrow(rb), estimate = c(fixef(m0)["compartmentPlantRoot"], fixef(m1)["compartmentPlantRoot"]),
   189	                  se = c(summary(m0)$coefficients["compartmentPlantRoot", 2], summary(m1)$coefficients["compartmentPlantRoot", 2]))
   190	# per-position DNA ratio and DNA-scaled (per-gram proxy) contrasts; product built from GE x DNA (descriptive only)
   191	dvis <- cells(transform(dd, dna_ = dna), "PlantRoot", "Deep", "dna_", eps = 0)
   192	a1b <- rbind(a1b, data.frame(model = "root_bulk_log2_DNA_ratio_median", n = nrow(dvis),
   193	                             estimate = median(dvis$dna_), se = NA))
   194	wr(a1b, "A1_root_effect_dna_adjusted.tsv")
   195	# aridity and DNA: site medians in bulk soil
   196	bs <- aggregate(cbind(ldna, l1 = log2(hydB_1l + EPS)) ~ site, dd[dd$compartment == "Deep", ], median)
   197	bs <- merge(bs, site_env, by = "site")
   198	a1c <- rbind(
   199	  data.frame(test = "bulk site log DNA vs log MAP (ERA5)", n_sites = nrow(bs),
   200	             rho = cor(bs$ldna, log(bs$map_era5_mm), method = "spearman"),
   201	             p = cor.test(bs$ldna, log(bs$map_era5_mm), method = "spearman", exact = FALSE)$p.value),
   202	  data.frame(test = "bulk site 1l vs log DNA", n_sites = nrow(bs),
   203	             rho = cor(bs$l1, bs$ldna, method = "spearman"),
   204	             p = cor.test(bs$l1, bs$ldna, method = "spearman", exact = FALSE)$p.value))
   205	x <- dd[dd$compartment == "Deep", ]; x$y <- log2(x$hydB_1l + EPS); x$lmap <- as.numeric(scale(log(site_env$map_era5_mm[match(x$site, site_env$site)])))
   206	f0 <- lmer(y ~ lmap + trip + dna_kit + (1 | site), data = x, REML = FALSE)
   207	f1 <- lmer(y ~ lmap + ldna + trip + dna_kit + (1 | site), data = x, REML = FALSE)
   208	a1c <- rbind(a1c, data.frame(test = c("bulk 1l per SD log MAP, DNA cohort", "bulk 1l per SD log MAP, + log DNA"),
   209	                             n_sites = length(unique(x$site)), rho = c(fixef(f0)["lmap"], fixef(f1)["lmap"]),
   210	                             p = c(summary(f0)$coefficients["lmap", 2], summary(f1)$coefficients["lmap", 2])))
   211	names(a1c)[3:4] <- c("estimate", "p_or_se")
   212	wr(a1c, "A1_aridity_and_dna.tsv")
   213	
   214	# ================================================================== CoverM-based lineage analyses (A2, A4, A6)
   215	ab <- rd(gzfile(file.path(IN, "coverm_rep_abundance_long.tsv.gz")), header = FALSE, col.names = c("sample", "rep", "abund"))
   216	ab <- ab[ab$rep != "unmapped" & is.finite(ab$abund) & ab$abund > 0 & ab$sample %in% d0$sample_id & !ab$rep %in% ORPHANS, ]
   217	mapped <- tapply(ab$abund, ab$sample, sum); ab$share <- ab$abund / as.numeric(mapped[ab$sample])
   218	cls <- rd(file.path(IN, "atmo_classification_v2.tsv")); cls <- cls[!cls$rep_id %in% ORPHANS, ]
   219	grp_reps <- function(g) unique(cls$rep_id[cls$marker == "hydB" & grepl(paste0("Group_", g, "$"), cls$assignment)])
   220	R1L <- grp_reps("1l"); R1H <- grp_reps("1h"); R2A <- grp_reps("2a")
   221	RFI <- setdiff(readLines(file.path(IN, "guild_coxL_reps_v2.txt")), ORPHANS)
   222	tax <- rbind(rd(file.path(IN, "r232_bac120.summary.tsv"))[, 1:2], rd(file.path(IN, "r232_ar53.summary.tsv"))[, 1:2])
   223	rk  <- function(cl, p) ifelse(grepl(paste0(p, "__[^;]"), cl), sub(paste0(".*", p, "__([^;]*).*"), "\\1", cl), NA)
   224	tax$phylum <- rk(tax$classification, "p"); tax$order <- rk(tax$classification, "o"); tax$genus <- rk(tax$classification, "g")
   225	ab$genus <- tax$genus[match(ab$rep, tax$user_genome)]; ab$genus[is.na(ab$genus)] <- paste0("unclassified_", ab$rep[is.na(ab$genus)])
   226	ab$c1l <- ab$rep %in% R1L
   227	sm <- merge(ab, meta[, c("sample_id", "site", "trip", "compartment")], by.x = "sample", by.y = "sample_id")
   228	sm$visit <- paste(sm$site, sm$trip, sep = "|")
   229	
   230	# A2 (a) decomposition of the root/bulk change in 1l-carrier share into genus-abundance and within-genus terms
   231	gv <- aggregate(cbind(share, s1l = share * c1l) ~ visit + compartment + genus, data = sm, FUN = sum)
   232	# per visit and position: mean over replicate samples of genus share and genus 1l share
   233	nrep <- aggregate(sample ~ visit + compartment, data = unique(sm[, c("sample", "visit", "compartment")]), FUN = length)
   234	gv <- merge(gv, nrep, by = c("visit", "compartment")); gv$w <- gv$share / gv$sample; gv$w1 <- gv$s1l / gv$sample
   235	dec <- NULL
   236	for (v in unique(gv$visit)) {
   237	  r <- gv[gv$visit == v & gv$compartment == "PlantRoot", ]; b <- gv[gv$visit == v & gv$compartment == "Deep", ]
   238	  if (!nrow(r) || !nrow(b)) next
   239	  g <- merge(r[, c("genus", "w", "w1")], b[, c("genus", "w", "w1")], by = "genus", all = TRUE, suffixes = c("_r", "_b"))
   240	  g[is.na(g)] <- 0
   241	  q_r <- ifelse(g$w_r > 0, g$w1_r / g$w_r, NA); q_b <- ifelse(g$w_b > 0, g$w1_b / g$w_b, NA)
   242	  q_r[is.na(q_r)] <- q_b[is.na(q_r)]; q_b[is.na(q_b)] <- q_r[is.na(q_b)]; q_r[is.na(q_r)] <- 0; q_b[is.na(q_b)] <- 0
   243	  tot_r <- sum(g$w1_r); tot_b <- sum(g$w1_b)
   244	  # Bennet decomposition: delta = sum(dw * qbar) + sum(wbar * dq)
   245	  dec <- rbind(dec, data.frame(visit = v, share1l_root = tot_r, share1l_bulk = tot_b,
   246	    delta = tot_r - tot_b, genus_turnover = sum((g$w_r - g$w_b) * (q_r + q_b) / 2),
   247	    within_genus = sum((g$w_r + g$w_b) / 2 * (q_r - q_b))))
   248	}
   249	dec$site <- sub("\\|.*", "", dec$visit)
   250	a2a <- data.frame(n_visits = nrow(dec), median_share1l_bulk = median(dec$share1l_bulk), median_share1l_root = median(dec$share1l_root),
   251	                  median_delta = median(dec$delta), median_genus_turnover = median(dec$genus_turnover),
   252	                  median_within_genus = median(dec$within_genus),
   253	                  frac_delta_from_turnover = sum(dec$genus_turnover) / sum(dec$delta),
   254	                  p_within_genus_site = { s <- tapply(dec$within_genus, dec$site, mean); wilcox.test(s, exact = FALSE)$p.value })
   255	wr(dec, "A2_root_bulk_decomposition_by_visit.tsv"); wr(a2a, "A2_root_bulk_decomposition_summary.tsv")
   256	
   257	# A2 (b) aridity: across bulk sites, genus-weight term vs within-genus term for the 1l-carrier share
   258	bulk <- gv[gv$compartment == "Deep", ]; bulk$site <- as.integer(sub("\\|.*", "", bulk$visit))
   259	bsg <- aggregate(cbind(w, w1) ~ site + genus, data = bulk, FUN = mean)
   260	qbar <- aggregate(cbind(w, w1) ~ genus, data = bsg, FUN = mean); qbar$q <- ifelse(qbar$w > 0, qbar$w1 / qbar$w, 0)
   261	bsg$qbar <- qbar$q[match(bsg$genus, qbar$genus)]
   262	site_dec <- aggregate(cbind(obs = w1, turnover_only = w * qbar) ~ site, data = bsg, FUN = sum)
   263	site_dec <- merge(site_dec, site_env[, c("site", "map_era5_mm")], by = "site")
   264	a2b <- data.frame(n_sites = nrow(site_dec),
   265	  rho_observed_vs_logMAP = cor(site_dec$obs, log(site_dec$map_era5_mm), method = "spearman"),
   266	  rho_turnover_only_vs_logMAP = cor(site_dec$turnover_only, log(site_dec$map_era5_mm), method = "spearman"),
   267	  rho_within_residual_vs_logMAP = cor(site_dec$obs - site_dec$turnover_only, log(site_dec$map_era5_mm), method = "spearman"),
   268	  p_within_residual = cor.test(site_dec$obs - site_dec$turnover_only, log(site_dec$map_era5_mm), method = "spearman", exact = FALSE)$p.value)
   269	# within mixed genera: carrier fraction vs log MAP (genera with carriers and non-carriers in the catalogue)
   270	gen_reps <- split(tax$user_genome, tax$genus)
   271	mixed <- names(Filter(function(r) { r <- intersect(r, unique(ab$rep)); any(r %in% R1L) && any(!r %in% R1L) }, gen_reps))
   272	mg <- bsg[bsg$genus %in% mixed & bsg$w > 0, ]; mg$q <- mg$w1 / mg$w
   273	mg$lmap <- as.numeric(scale(log(site_env$map_era5_mm[match(mg$site, site_env$site)])))
   274	fm <- lmer(qlogis(pmin(pmax(q, 0.005), 0.995)) ~ lmap + (1 | genus) + (1 | site), data = mg, weights = sqrt(w), REML = FALSE)
   275	a2b$n_mixed_genera <- length(unique(mg$genus)); a2b$within_mixed_genera_logit_slope_per_SD_logMAP <- fixef(fm)["lmap"]
   276	a2b$within_mixed_genera_se <- summary(fm)$coefficients["lmap", 2]
   277	a2b$mixed_genera_share_of_bulk_1l <- sum(bsg$w1[bsg$genus %in% mixed]) / sum(bsg$w1)
   278	wr(site_dec, "A2_aridity_site_decomposition.tsv"); wr(a2b, "A2_aridity_decomposition_summary.tsv")
   279	
   280	# A6 carrier representation vs copies per carrier (1l)
   281	ncopy <- table(cls$rep_id[cls$marker == "hydB" & grepl("Group_1l$", cls$assignment)])
   282	sm$copies <- as.numeric(ncopy[sm$rep]); sm$copies[is.na(sm$copies)] <- 0
   283	cp <- aggregate(cbind(F = share * c1l, M = share * copies) ~ sample + visit + compartment + site + trip, data = sm, FUN = sum)
   284	cp$C <- ifelse(cp$F > 0, cp$M / cp$F, NA)
   285	cpc <- cells(cp[is.finite(cp$C), ], "PlantRoot", "Deep", c("F", "M", "C"), eps = 1e-6)
   286	cps <- cells(cp[is.finite(cp$C), ], "Surface", "Deep", c("F", "M", "C"), eps = 1e-6)
   287	wr(rbind(contrast_table(cpc, c("F", "M", "C"), "root/bulk"), contrast_table(cps, c("F", "M", "C"), "surface/bulk")),
   288	   "A6_copy_number_vs_carrier_representation.tsv")
   289	
   290	# A4 CBB co-carriage (RuBisCO large + small subunit + phosphoribulokinase) by hydrogenase group
   291	kt <- rd(file.path(IN, "rep_cbb_ko.tsv")); kt <- kt[!kt$rep %in% ORPHANS, ]
   292	kt$cbb <- as.integer(kt$K01601 == 1 & kt$K01602 == 1 & kt$K00855 == 1)
   293	kt$group <- ifelse(kt$rep %in% R1L & kt$rep %in% R1H, "1l+1h", ifelse(kt$rep %in% R1L, "1l", ifelse(kt$rep %in% R1H, "1h",
   294	            ifelse(kt$rep %in% R2A, "2a_only", "no_high_affinity_or_2a"))))
   295	kt$order <- tax$order[match(kt$rep, tax$user_genome)]; kt$phylum <- tax$phylum[match(kt$rep, tax$user_genome)]
   296	gs <- if (file.exists(file.path(IN, "rep_genome_size.tsv"))) rd(file.path(IN, "rep_genome_size.tsv")) else NULL
   297	a4 <- aggregate(cbind(n = 1, cbb) ~ group, data = kt, FUN = sum); a4$frac_cbb <- a4$cbb / a4$n
   298	k2 <- kt[kt$group %in% c("1l", "1h") & !is.na(kt$order), ]; k2$is1l <- as.integer(k2$group == "1l")
   299	gm <- glmer(cbb ~ is1l + (1 | order), data = k2, family = binomial)
   300	a4$within_order_log_odds_1l_vs_1h <- fixef(gm)["is1l"]; a4$se <- summary(gm)$coefficients["is1l", 2]
   301	a4$p <- summary(gm)$coefficients["is1l", 4]
   302	if (!is.null(gs)) {
   303	  k2$comp <- gs$completeness[match(k2$rep, gs$genome_id)]
   304	  gm2 <- glmer(cbb ~ is1l + scale(comp) + (1 | order), data = k2, family = binomial)
   305	  a4$completeness_adjusted_log_odds <- fixef(gm2)["is1l"]; a4$completeness_adjusted_p <- summary(gm2)$coefficients["is1l", 4]
   306	}
   307	wr(a4, "A4_cbb_cocarriage_by_group.tsv")
   308	# abundance of 1l carriers with and without CBB: root contrast and aridity
   309	cbb_reps <- kt$rep[kt$cbb == 1]
   310	sm$sub <- ifelse(sm$c1l & sm$rep %in% cbb_reps, "l1_cbb", ifelse(sm$c1l, "l1_nocbb", "other"))
   311	sp <- aggregate(share ~ sample + visit + compartment + site + trip + sub, data = sm[sm$sub != "other", ], FUN = sum)
   312	sw <- reshape(sp, idvar = c("sample", "visit", "compartment", "site", "trip"), timevar = "sub", direction = "wide")
   313	sw[is.na(sw)] <- 0; names(sw) <- sub("share\\.", "", names(sw))
   314	sw$frac_cbb <- sw$l1_cbb / (sw$l1_cbb + sw$l1_nocbb)
   315	a4c <- rbind(contrast_table(cells(sw, "PlantRoot", "Deep", c("l1_cbb", "l1_nocbb"), eps = 1e-5), c("l1_cbb", "l1_nocbb"), "root/bulk"),
   316	             contrast_table(cells(sw, "Surface", "Deep", c("l1_cbb", "l1_nocbb"), eps = 1e-5), c("l1_cbb", "l1_nocbb"), "surface/bulk"))
   317	swb <- sw[sw$compartment == "Deep", ]; sb <- aggregate(cbind(frac_cbb, l1_cbb, l1_nocbb) ~ site, swb, median)
   318	sb <- merge(sb, site_env, by = "site")
   319	a4c <- rbind(a4c, data.frame(contrast = "bulk sites vs log MAP (Spearman rho in median_log2)", quantity = c("frac_cbb", "l1_cbb", "l1_nocbb"),
   320	  n_visits = NA, n_sites = nrow(sb), median_log2 = sapply(c("frac_cbb", "l1_cbb", "l1_nocbb"), function(v) cor(sb[[v]], log(sb$map_era5_mm), method = "spearman")),
   321	  ci_lo = NA, ci_hi = NA, frac_below0 = NA,
   322	  p_site_wilcoxon = sapply(c("frac_cbb", "l1_cbb", "l1_nocbb"), function(v) cor.test(sb[[v]], log(sb$map_era5_mm), method = "spearman", exact = FALSE)$p.value)))
   323	wr(a4c, "A4_cbb_subsets_position_and_aridity.tsv")
   324	
   325	# ================================================================== A3 surface filter vs exposure
   326	sx <- merge(cs, st_env, by = c("site", "trip"))
   327	a3 <- do.call(rbind, lapply(FOCAL, function(v) do.call(rbind, lapply(c("T_d30", "smap_sm_W30d", "P90_era5"), function(e) {
   328	  x <- sx[is.finite(sx[[e]]), ]; x$y <- x[[paste0("excess_", v)]]; x$e <- as.numeric(scale(if (e == "P90_era5") log1p(x[[e]]) else x[[e]]))
   329	  f1 <- lmer(y ~ e + trip + (1 | site), data = x, REML = FALSE); f0 <- lmer(y ~ trip + (1 | site), data = x, REML = FALSE)
   330	  data.frame(marker = v, exposure = e, n_visits = nrow(x), beta_per_SD = fixef(f1)["e"], se = summary(f1)$coefficients["e", 2],
   331	             LRT_p = anova(f0, f1)$`Pr(>Chisq)`[2])
   332	}))))
   333	a3$q_BH <- p.adjust(a3$LRT_p, "BH")
   334	wr(a3, "A3_surface_excess_vs_exposure.tsv")
   335	
   336	# ================================================================== H3 spatial (bulk site medians)
   337	prank <- function(x, y, Z) {
   338	  rx <- rank(x); ry <- rank(y); Zr <- apply(as.matrix(Z), 2, rank)
   339	  ex <- resid(lm(rx ~ Zr)); ey <- resid(lm(ry ~ Zr)); ct <- cor.test(ex, ey)
   340	  c(rho = unname(ct$estimate), p = ct$p.value)
   341	}
   342	xrf <- rd(file.path(IN, "xrf_site_compartment_panel.tsv")); xrf <- xrf[xrf$compartment == "deep", ]
   343	topo <- rd(file.path(IN, "topography.tsv"))
   344	site_env <- merge(site_env, xrf[, c("site", "Ca", "Cl", "S", "Fe", "Al", "Si", "K", "Na")], by = "site", all.x = TRUE)
   345	site_env <- merge(site_env, topo[, c("site", "twi", "slope_deg")], by = "site", all.x = TRUE)
   346	# geochemical axes: PCA of centred log-ratio XRF (major elements, zeros replaced by half the minimum positive)
   347	el <- c("Al", "Ca", "Cl", "Fe", "K", "Mg", "Na", "P", "S", "Si", "Ti", "Mn", "Sr", "Zr")
   348	xm <- as.matrix(xrf[, el]); for (j in seq_len(ncol(xm))) { z <- xm[, j]; z[z <= 0] <- min(z[z > 0]) / 2; xm[, j] <- z }
   349	clr <- log(xm) - rowMeans(log(xm)); pca <- prcomp(clr, scale. = TRUE)
   350	geo <- data.frame(site = xrf$site, geoPC1 = pca$x[, 1], geoPC2 = pca$x[, 2])
   351	site_env <- merge(site_env, geo, by = "site", all.x = TRUE)
   352	# community-composition axis (bulk site means of CoverM profiles)
   353	deep_ids <- meta$sample_id[meta$compartment == "Deep"]
   354	cm <- xtabs(abund ~ sample + rep, data = ab[ab$sample %in% deep_ids, ]); cm <- unclass(cm); cm <- cm / rowSums(cm)
   355	sid <- meta$site[match(rownames(cm), meta$sample_id)]
   356	cm_site <- rowsum(cm, sid) / as.numeric(table(sid)[rownames(rowsum(cm, sid))])
   357	site_env$comp_pc1_deep <- cmdscale(vegdist(cm_site, "bray"), k = 1)[, 1][as.character(site_env$site)]
   358	
   359	blocks <- function(k) { set.seed(k); kmeans(scale(site_env[, c("lat", "lon")]), centers = k, nstart = 50)$cluster }
   360	for (k in c(4, 6, 8, 10)) site_env[[paste0("block", k)]] <- blocks(k)
   361	h3 <- NULL
   362	for (comp in c("Deep", "Surface", "PlantRoot")) for (v in FOCAL) {
   363	  x <- d0[d0$compartment == comp, ]
   364	  s <- aggregate(x[[v]], list(site = x$site), median); names(s)[2] <- "ge"
   365	  s <- merge(s, site_env, by = "site")
   366	  for (pv in c("map_era5_mm", "map_power_mm", "map_bestmatch_mm")) {
   367	    r0 <- cor.test(s$ge, s[[pv]], method = "spearman", exact = FALSE)
   368	    pg <- prank(s$ge, s[[pv]], s[, c("lat", "lon", "elevation_m")])
   369	    sc <- s[is.finite(s$comp_pc1_deep), ]; pc <- prank(sc$ge, sc[[pv]], sc[, "comp_pc1_deep", drop = FALSE])
   370	    sx2 <- s[is.finite(s$geoPC1) & is.finite(s$Ca), ]
   371	    pgeo <- prank(sx2$ge, sx2[[pv]], sx2[, c("geoPC1", "geoPC2")]); psalt <- prank(sx2$ge, sx2[[pv]], sx2[, c("Ca", "Cl", "S")])
   372	    bb <- sapply(c(4, 6, 8, 10), function(k) { bk <- s[[paste0("block", k)]]
   373	      q95(replicate(NB, { b <- sample(unique(bk), replace = TRUE)
   374	        z <- do.call(rbind, lapply(b, function(j) s[bk == j, ])); suppressWarnings(cor(z$ge, z[[pv]], method = "spearman")) })) })
   375	    # residual spatial autocorrelation of rank regression (Moran's I, inverse distance)
   376	    rr <- resid(lm(rank(s$ge) ~ rank(s[[pv]])))
   377	    dm <- as.matrix(dist(cbind(s$lon * cos(mean(s$lat) * pi / 180), s$lat))); w <- 1 / dm; w[!is.finite(w)] <- 0; diag(w) <- 0
   378	    mi <- Moran.I(rr, w)
   379	    h3 <- rbind(h3, data.frame(compartment = comp, marker = v, precip = pv, n_sites = nrow(s),
   380	      rho = unname(r0$estimate), p = r0$p.value,
   381	      block4_ci = sprintf("%.2f to %.2f", bb[1, 1], bb[2, 1]), block6_ci = sprintf("%.2f to %.2f", bb[1, 2], bb[2, 2]),
   382	      block8_ci = sprintf("%.2f to %.2f", bb[1, 3], bb[2, 3]), block10_ci = sprintf("%.2f to %.2f", bb[1, 4], bb[2, 4]),
   383	      moran_I_resid = mi$observed, moran_p = mi$p.value,
   384	      partial_rho_latlonelev = pg[["rho"]], partial_p_latlonelev = pg[["p"]],
   385	      partial_rho_geoPC12 = pgeo[["rho"]], partial_p_geoPC12 = pgeo[["p"]],
   386	      partial_rho_CaClS = psalt[["rho"]], partial_p_CaClS = psalt[["p"]],
   387	      partial_rho_compPC1 = pc[["rho"]], partial_p_compPC1 = pc[["p"]]))
   388	  }
   389	}
   390	h3$q_BH_family <- ave(h3$p, h3$precip, FUN = function(p) p.adjust(p, "BH"))
   391	wr(h3, "H3_spatial_by_marker_position.tsv")
   392	x3 <- merge(d0, site_env[, c("site", "map_era5_mm")], by = "site")
   393	h3mm <- do.call(rbind, lapply(FOCAL, function(v) {
   394	  x3$y <- log2(x3[[v]] + EPS); x3$lmap <- as.numeric(scale(log(x3$map_era5_mm)))
   395	  f1 <- lmer(y ~ compartment + trip + lmap + (1 | site) + (1 | visit), data = x3, REML = FALSE)
   396	  f0 <- lmer(y ~ compartment + trip + (1 | site) + (1 | visit), data = x3, REML = FALSE)
   397	  data.frame(marker = v, beta_per_SD_logMAP = fixef(f1)[["lmap"]], se = summary(f1)$coefficients["lmap", 2],
   398	             LRT_p = anova(f0, f1)$`Pr(>Chisq)`[2])
   399	}))
   400	wr(h3mm, "H3_mixed_model_logMAP.tsv")
   401	
   402	# ================================================================== H3 temporal: within/between decomposition (R1-03, R1-04, R1-11)
   403	EQB <- 0.5   # equivalence bound: |change| < 0.5 log2 across the within-site IQR of the exposure
   404	h3t <- NULL
   405	for (comp in c("Deep", "Surface", "PlantRoot")) for (v in FOCAL) for (pv in c("P365_era5", "P90_era5", "P365_bestmatch")) {
   406	  x <- d0[d0$compartment == comp, ]
   407	  cc <- aggregate(log2(x[[v]] + EPS), list(site = x$site, trip = x$trip), median); names(cc)[3] <- "y"
   408	  cc <- merge(cc, st_env, by = c("site", "trip")); cc <- cc[is.finite(cc[[pv]]), ]
   409	  cc$lp <- log1p(cc[[pv]])
   410	  cc <- cc[cc$site %in% names(which(table(cc$site) >= 2)), ]
   411	  cc$lp_between <- ave(cc$lp, cc$site); cc$lp_within <- cc$lp - cc$lp_between
   412	  f <- lmer(y ~ lp_within + lp_between + trip + (1 | site), data = cc, REML = FALSE)
   413	  f0 <- lmer(y ~ lp_between + trip + (1 | site), data = cc, REML = FALSE)
   414	  b <- summary(f)$coefficients["lp_within", 1:2]
   415	  iqr <- IQR(cc$lp_within)
   416	  bt <- q95(replicate(NB, { s <- sample(unique(cc$site), replace = TRUE)
   417	    w <- do.call(rbind, lapply(seq_along(s), function(i) transform(cc[cc$site == s[i], ], site = paste0(s[i], "_", i))))
   418	    tryCatch(fixef(lmer(y ~ lp_within + lp_between + trip + (1 | site), data = w, REML = FALSE))[["lp_within"]], error = function(e) NA) }))
   419	  h3t <- rbind(h3t, data.frame(compartment = comp, marker = v, exposure = pv, n_visits = nrow(cc), n_sites = length(unique(cc$site)),
   420	    trips = paste(sort(unique(cc$trip)), collapse = ","), date_range = paste(range(cc$trip_date), collapse = " to "),
   421	    within_slope = b[1], within_se = b[2], within_LRT_p = anova(f0, f)$`Pr(>Chisq)`[2],
   422	    within_boot_lo = bt[1], within_boot_hi = bt[2], within_IQR = iqr,
   423	    change_across_IQR_lo = bt[1] * iqr, change_across_IQR_hi = bt[2] * iqr,
   424	    equivalent_within_0.5 = (abs(bt[1] * iqr) < EQB & abs(bt[2] * iqr) < EQB),
   425	    between_slope = fixef(f)[["lp_between"]]))
   426	}
   427	h3t$q_BH_family <- ave(h3t$within_LRT_p, h3t$exposure, FUN = function(p) p.adjust(p, "BH"))
   428	wr(h3t, "H3_temporal_within_between.tsv")
   429	
   430	# ================================================================== stability (R1-15, R1-16)
   431	pm_out <- NULL
   432	for (comp in c("all", "Deep")) {
   433	  ids <- if (comp == "all") d0$sample_id else d0$sample_id[d0$compartment == "Deep"]
   434	  m <- xtabs(abund ~ sample + rep, data = ab[ab$sample %in% ids, ]); m <- unclass(m); m <- m / rowSums(m)
   435	  md <- d0[match(rownames(m), d0$sample_id), ]
   436	  bc <- vegdist(m, "bray")
   437	  # trip tested with permutations restricted within site; site tested with permutations of whole sites (free)
   438	  ctrl_trip <- how(nperm = 999, blocks = factor(md$site))
   439	  f <- if (comp == "all") bc ~ factor(trip) + factor(site) + compartment else bc ~ factor(trip) + factor(site)
   440	  a_all <- adonis2(f, data = md, by = "margin", permutations = 999)
   441	  a_trip <- adonis2(f, data = md, by = "margin", permutations = ctrl_trip)
   442	  disp <- permutest(betadisper(bc, md$trip), permutations = 999)
   443	  pm_out <- rbind(pm_out, data.frame(subset = comp, term = rownames(a_all), Df = a_all$Df, R2 = a_all$R2,
   444	    R2_per_df = a_all$R2 / a_all$Df, p_free = a_all$`Pr(>F)`,
   445	    p_trip_within_site = ifelse(rownames(a_all) == "factor(trip)", a_trip$`Pr(>F)`[rownames(a_trip) == "factor(trip)"], NA),
   446	    trip_dispersion_p = disp$tab$`Pr(>F)`[1], row.names = NULL))
   447	}
   448	wr(pm_out, "stability_permanova_restricted.tsv")
   449	# synchrony of 1l-carrier genera across trips within bulk soil only, sites with >= 4 trips
   450	car <- sm[sm$c1l & sm$compartment == "Deep", ]
   451	gt <- aggregate(share ~ site + trip + genus, data = car, FUN = mean)
   452	syn <- do.call(rbind, lapply(split(gt, gt$site), function(x) {
   453	  w <- xtabs(share ~ trip + genus, data = x); w <- unclass(w)
   454	  if (nrow(w) < 4 || ncol(w) < 2) return(NULL)
   455	  phi <- function(w) var(rowSums(w)) / sum(apply(w, 2, sd))^2
   456	  null <- replicate(199, phi(apply(w, 2, sample)))
   457	  data.frame(site = x$site[1], n_trips = nrow(w), n_genera = ncol(w), phi = phi(w), phi_null_median = median(null))
   458	}))
   459	wr(syn, "stability_synchrony_bulk_1l_genera.tsv")
   460	
   461	# ================================================================== sensitivity of headline estimates
   462	variants <- list(primary = list(file = "guild_genome_equivalents_v2.tsv", floor = 60, drop = character()),
   463	                 id50 = list(file = "guild_genome_equivalents_v2.tsv", floor = 50, drop = character()),
   464	                 id70 = list(file = "guild_genome_equivalents_v2.tsv", floor = 70, drop = character()),
   465	                 without_trip5 = list(file = "guild_genome_equivalents_v2.tsv", floor = 60, drop = "trip5"),
   466	                 without_trip4 = list(file = "guild_genome_equivalents_v2.tsv", floor = 60, drop = "trip4"))
   467	for (f in c("guild_genome_equivalents_v2_noflagged.tsv", "guild_genome_equivalents_v2_strict1l.tsv"))
   468	  if (file.exists(file.path(RD, f))) variants[[sub("guild_genome_equivalents_v2_", "", sub(".tsv", "", f))]] <- list(file = f, floor = 60, drop = character())
   469	sens <- do.call(rbind, lapply(names(variants), function(nm) {
   470	  v <- variants[[nm]]; d <- load_ge(v$file, v$floor, v$drop)
   471	  r <- add_excess(cells(d, "PlantRoot", "Deep", MARKERS)); s <- add_excess(cells(d, "Surface", "Deep", MARKERS))
   472	  b <- aggregate(d$hydB_1l[d$compartment == "Deep"], list(site = d$site[d$compartment == "Deep"]), median)
   473	  b <- merge(b, site_env, by = "site")
   474	  data.frame(variant = nm, n_samples = nrow(d), root_1l = median(r$hydB_1l), root_excess_1l = median(r$excess_hydB_1l),
   475	             root_excess_1l_p = site_wilcox(r, "excess_hydB_1l"), root_excess_1h = median(r$excess_hydB_1h),
   476	             root_excess_formI = median(r$excess_coxL_formI), surface_excess_1l = median(s$excess_hydB_1l),
   477	             surface_excess_formI = median(s$excess_coxL_formI),
   478	             rho_bulk_1l_ERA5 = cor(b$x, b$map_era5_mm, method = "spearman"),
   479	             rho_bulk_1l_POWER = cor(b$x, b$map_power_mm, method = "spearman"))
   480	}))
   481	wr(sens, "sensitivity.tsv")
   482	
   483	sink(file.path(OUT, "SUMMARY.txt"))
   484	cat("Life on air (P3), revised analyses (h123_v2.R), sites 1-60, GE v2 at 60% identity\n")
   485	cat(sprintf("samples %d (surface %d, bulk %d, root-adjacent %d) at %d sites; with DNA yield %d\n", nrow(d0),
   486	    sum(d0$compartment == "Surface"), sum(d0$compartment == "Deep"), sum(d0$compartment == "PlantRoot"),
   487	    length(unique(d0$site)), sum(is.finite(d0$dna))))
   488	cat("\nbulk medians (GE):\n"); print(round(sapply(MARKERS, function(v) median(d0[d0$compartment == "Deep", v])), 3))
   489	for (nm in c("contr", "pc_sens", "h1_trip", "jm", "pos_eff", "a1", "a1b", "a1c", "a2a", "a2b", "a4", "a4c", "a3",
   490	             "h3", "h3mm", "h3t", "pm_out", "sens")) { cat("\n==", nm, "==\n"); print(get(nm), row.names = FALSE, digits = 3) }
   491	cat(sprintf("\nsynchrony (bulk, 1l genera): median phi %.3f vs null %.3f at %d sites\n", median(syn$phi), median(syn$phi_null_median), nrow(syn)))
   492	sink()
   493	cat(readLines(file.path(OUT, "SUMMARY.txt")), sep = "\n")

     1	#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
     2	# Life on air (P3): mechanism proxies for the root-adjacent depletion of group 1l, revised after review round 1
     3	# (12 Oct 2026; supersedes h1_mechanism.R). Changes: sites 1-60, GE v2 (corrected CoxL forms, group 2a separate),
     4	# visit random effects (R1-12), 2a carriage in the trait models, genus-model sensitivity to sparse genera and
     5	# single-representative genera (R1-22); "growth" is gRodon2 predicted maximal growth potential (R1-33).
     6	# Li et al. 2023 (ISME J) attribute the loss of trace-gas oxidisers under desert shrubs to organic carbon
     7	# favouring copiotrophs. Soil organic carbon was never measured in this campaign (P1 has XRF, pH and
     8	# weather only), so two proxies are tested:
     9	#   M1 plant input: NDVI (60-day window, site x trip) against 1l per compartment and against the root effect.
    10	#   M2 copiotrophy: gRodon2 predicted minimum doubling time per representative (g1_growth_rate.sh).
    11	#      a  are 1l carriers slower growers than non-carriers within order?
    12	#      b  is the abundance-weighted community doubling time shorter beside roots (paired site x trip)?
    13	#      c  does the per-pair 1l excess loss scale with that shift?
    14	#      d  genus level: does carrying 1l predict root depletion beyond growth rate?
    15	# Capacity, not activity. Run from the repository root:
    16	#   micromamba run -n eq-lifeonair Rscript lifeonair/code/h1_mechanism.R
    17	
    18	suppressMessages(library(lme4))
    19	IN <- "lifeonair/results/inputs"; OUT <- "lifeonair/results/h1_mechanism_v2"
    20	dir.create(OUT, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)

    22	wt <- function(x, f) write.table(x, file.path(OUT, f), sep = "\t", quote = FALSE, row.names = FALSE)
    23	sp <- function(x, y) { ok <- is.finite(x) & is.finite(y); r <- cor.test(x[ok], y[ok], method = "spearman", exact = FALSE)
    24	  c(rho = unname(r$estimate), p = r$p.value, n = sum(ok)) }

    48	m1b <- data.frame(test = c("pair excess_1l ~ NDVI (spearman)", "site-median excess_1l ~ NDVI (spearman)", "pair excess_1l ~ NDVI (lmer, per SD)"),
    49	                  estimate = c(sp(cp$excess_1l, cp$ndvi_W60d)[1], sp(s_cp$excess_1l, s_cp$ndvi_W60d)[1], mm[2, 1]),

    66	gr$phylum <- rk(tax$classification, "p")[match(gr$rep, tax$user_genome)]

    78	                  median_d_noncarriers = c(NA, median(gr$d_hours[!gr$c1l]), median(gr$d_hours[!gr$c1h]), median(gr$d_hours[!gr$c2a]), median(gr$d_hours[!gr$cfI])))
    79	wt(m2a, "m2a_doubling_time_by_carrier_status.tsv"); print(m2a, digits = 3)
    80	say("M2a 1l carriers: log doubling time %+.3f within order (SE %.3f); medians %.1f vs %.1f h",
    81	    fa[2, 1], fa[2, 2], m2a$median_d_carriers[2], m2a$median_d_noncarriers[2])
    82	
    83	# b community-weighted mean log doubling time per sample (CoverM, reps with an estimate)
    84	ab <- rd(gzfile(file.path(IN, "coverm_rep_abundance_long.tsv.gz")), header = FALSE, col.names = c("sample", "rep", "abund"))
    85	ab <- ab[ab$rep != "unmapped" & is.finite(ab$abund) & ab$abund > 0 & !ab$rep %in% ORPHANS, ]
    86	ab$logd <- gr$logd[match(ab$rep, gr$rep)]
    87	cov_frac <- tapply((!is.na(ab$logd)) * ab$abund, ab$sample, sum) / tapply(ab$abund, ab$sample, sum)
    88	ab <- ab[!is.na(ab$logd), ]
    89	cwm <- data.frame(sample_id = names(cov_frac), cwm_logd = as.numeric(tapply(ab$logd * ab$abund, ab$sample, sum)[names(cov_frac)] /
    90	                                                     tapply(ab$abund, ab$sample, sum)[names(cov_frac)]),
    91	                  covered = as.numeric(cov_frac))
    92	d2 <- merge(d, cwm, by = "sample_id")
    93	say("M2b CWM coverage of mapped abundance: median %.2f", median(d2$covered))
    94	cells <- function(x, a, b, v) { k <- paste(x$site, x$trip); out <- NULL
    95	  for (z in unique(k)) { p <- x[k == z & x$compartment == a, ]; q <- x[k == z & x$compartment == b, ]
    96	    if (nrow(p) && nrow(q)) out <- rbind(out, data.frame(site = p$site[1], trip = p$trip[1], diff = median(p[[v]]) - median(q[[v]]))) }
    97	  out }
    98	db <- cells(d2, "PlantRoot", "Deep", "cwm_logd"); ds <- cells(d2, "Surface", "Deep", "cwm_logd")
    99	sb <- aggregate(diff ~ site, db, median); ss <- aggregate(diff ~ site, ds, median)
   100	m2b <- data.frame(contrast = c("PlantRoot - Deep", "Surface - Deep"), n_pairs = c(nrow(db), nrow(ds)),
   101	                  median_diff_logd = c(median(db$diff), median(ds$diff)),
   102	                  frac_pairs_faster = c(mean(db$diff < 0), mean(ds$diff < 0)),
   103	                  site_wilcox_p = c(wilcox.test(sb$diff)$p.value, wilcox.test(ss$diff)$p.value))
   104	wt(m2b, "m2b_community_doubling_time_shift.tsv"); print(m2b, digits = 3)
   105	
   106	# c per-pair 1l excess loss vs the copiotrophy shift
   107	pc <- merge(c1, setNames(db, c("site", "trip", "d_cwm_logd")), by = c("site", "trip"))
   108	mc <- summary(lmer(excess_1l ~ d_cwm_logd + (1 | site) + (1 | trip), pc))$coefficients
   109	# site bootstrap of the intercept (predicted excess at no shift) and slope
   110	bt <- replicate(1000, { s <- sample(unique(pc$site), replace = TRUE)
   111	  w <- do.call(rbind, lapply(seq_along(s), function(i) transform(pc[pc$site == s[i], ], site = paste0(s[i], "_", i))))
   112	  tryCatch(fixef(lmer(excess_1l ~ d_cwm_logd + (1 | site) + (1 | trip), w)), error = function(e) c(NA, NA)) })
   113	bt_ci <- apply(bt, 1, quantile, c(.025, .975), na.rm = TRUE)
   114	mc2 <- summary(lmer(hydB_1l ~ d_cwm_logd + (1 | site) + (1 | trip), pc))$coefficients
   115	m2c <- data.frame(response = c("excess_1l", "excess_1l", "hydB_1l log2FC"),
   116	                  test = c("spearman", "lmer slope per log-unit", "lmer slope per log-unit"),
   117	                  estimate = c(sp(pc$excess_1l, pc$d_cwm_logd)[1], mc[2, 1], mc2[2, 1]),
   118	                  p_or_se = c(sp(pc$excess_1l, pc$d_cwm_logd)[2], mc[2, 2], mc2[2, 2]), n = nrow(pc),
   119	                  intercept_at_no_shift = c(NA, mc[1, 1], mc2[1, 1]),
   120	                  intercept_boot95 = c(NA, sprintf("%.2f to %.2f", bt_ci[1, 1], bt_ci[2, 1]), NA),
   121	                  slope_boot95 = c(NA, sprintf("%.2f to %.2f", bt_ci[1, 2], bt_ci[2, 2]), NA))
   122	wt(m2c, "m2c_excess_loss_vs_copiotrophy_shift.tsv"); print(m2c, digits = 3)
   123	wt(merge(pc[, c("site", "trip", "hydB_1l", "excess_1l", "d_cwm_logd")], env[, c("site", "trip", "ndvi_W60d")],
   124	         by = c("site", "trip"), all.x = TRUE), "m_pairs_plantroot_vs_deep.tsv")   # per-pair table for the figure
   125	
   126	# d genus level: root depletion ~ growth rate + 1l carriage
   127	mapped <- tapply(ab$abund, ab$sample, sum)
   128	ab$genus <- gr$genus[match(ab$rep, gr$rep)]
   129	gm <- xtabs(abund ~ sample + genus, ab); gm <- sweep(as.matrix(unclass(gm)), 1, rowSums(gm), "/") * 100
   130	gm <- as.data.frame(gm); gm$sample_id <- rownames(gm); gm <- merge(meta, gm, by = "sample_id")
   131	gen <- setdiff(names(gm), names(meta))
   132	deep_mean <- colMeans(gm[gm$compartment == "Deep", gen]); gen <- gen[deep_mean >= 0.05]
   133	k <- paste(gm$site, gm$trip); keys <- intersect(k[gm$compartment == "PlantRoot"], k[gm$compartment == "Deep"])
   134	lfc <- sapply(gen, function(g) median(sapply(keys, function(z)
   135	  log2((median(gm[[g]][k == z & gm$compartment == "PlantRoot"]) + .01) / (median(gm[[g]][k == z & gm$compartment == "Deep"]) + .01)))))
   136	gt <- data.frame(genus = gen, deep_mean_pct = deep_mean[gen], log2fc_root = lfc,
   137	                 median_logd = tapply(gr$logd, gr$genus, median)[gen],
   138	                 frac_1l = tapply(gr$c1l, gr$genus, mean)[gen], frac_1h = tapply(gr$c1h, gr$genus, mean)[gen],
   139	                 frac_2a = tapply(gr$c2a, gr$genus, mean)[gen],
   140	                 frac_formI = tapply(gr$cfI, gr$genus, mean)[gen], n_reps = as.numeric(table(gr$genus)[gen]))
   141	gt$phylum <- sub(";.*", "", gt$genus)
   142	wt(gt, "m2d_genus_root_depletion.tsv")
   143	fit0 <- lm(log2fc_root ~ median_logd, gt, weights = sqrt(deep_mean_pct))
   144	fit1 <- lm(log2fc_root ~ median_logd + frac_1l + frac_1h + frac_2a + frac_formI, gt, weights = sqrt(deep_mean_pct))
   145	fit2 <- lm(log2fc_root ~ median_logd + frac_1l + frac_1h + frac_2a + frac_formI + phylum, gt, weights = sqrt(deep_mean_pct))
   146	# sensitivity (R1-22): drop genera with an exactly zero median change; keep genera with >= 2 representatives
   147	gnz <- gt[gt$log2fc_root != 0, ]; g2 <- gt[gt$n_reps >= 2, ]
   148	fit3 <- lm(log2fc_root ~ median_logd + frac_1l + frac_1h + frac_2a + frac_formI + phylum, gnz, weights = sqrt(deep_mean_pct))
   149	fit4 <- lm(log2fc_root ~ median_logd + frac_1l + frac_1h + frac_2a + frac_formI + phylum, g2, weights = sqrt(deep_mean_pct))
   150	co <- function(f, nm) { s <- summary(f)$coefficients; s <- s[intersect(c("median_logd", "frac_1l", "frac_1h", "frac_2a", "frac_formI"), rownames(s)), , drop = FALSE]
   151	  data.frame(model = nm, term = rownames(s), s, r2 = summary(f)$r.squared, n_genera = nrow(f$model), row.names = NULL) }
   152	m2d <- rbind(co(fit0, "growth only"), co(fit1, "growth + carriage"), co(fit2, "growth + carriage + phylum"),
   153	             co(fit3, "phylum model, nonzero change only"), co(fit4, "phylum model, genera with >=2 reps"))
   154	wt(m2d, "m2d_genus_models.tsv"); print(m2d, digits = 3)
   155	
   156	say("M2b root-adjacent CWM log doubling time shift %+.3f (%.0f%% of %d pairs faster; site p %.2g)",
   157	    m2b$median_diff_logd[1], 100 * m2b$frac_pairs_faster[1], m2b$n_pairs[1], m2b$site_wilcox_p[1])
   158	say("M2c excess_1l vs shift: spearman rho %.2f (p %.2g); lmer slope %.2f (SE %.2f), intercept at no shift %.2f",
   159	    m2c$estimate[1], m2c$p_or_se[1], m2c$estimate[2], m2c$p_or_se[2], m2c$intercept_at_no_shift[2])
   160	f2 <- m2d[m2d$model == "growth + carriage + phylum", ]
   161	say("M2d genus (n=%d): growth slope %.2f (p %.2g); frac_1l %.2f (p %.2g) with phylum",
   162	    nrow(gt), f2$Estimate[f2$term == "median_logd"], f2$Pr...t..[f2$term == "median_logd"],
   163	    f2$Estimate[f2$term == "frac_1l"], f2$Pr...t..[f2$term == "frac_1l"])
   164	writeLines(log_lines, file.path(OUT, "SUMMARY.txt"))
     1	#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
     2	# Life on air (P3): genomic traits of atmospheric-guild carriers (review round 1, consultation A5).
     3	# Do group 1l carriers differ from 1h carriers (and non-carriers) in estimated genome size, GC content or
     4	# gRodon doubling time, within order? Genome size is completeness-adjusted (length / completeness, CheckM2),
     5	# from IBEX 06_atmospheric/lifeonair_review_r1 job 53611804 (results/inputs/rep_genome_size.tsv).
     6	#   Rscript lifeonair/code/a5_genome_traits.R   -> lifeonair/results/a5_genome_traits/
     7	suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(lme4))
     8	IN <- "lifeonair/results/inputs"; OUT <- "lifeonair/results/a5_genome_traits"; dir.create(OUT, showWarnings = FALSE)
     9	rd <- function(f, ...) read.delim(f, check.names = FALSE, quote = "", ...)
    10	ORPHANS <- readLines("lifeonair/results/reads/orphan_sub_reps.txt")
    11	gs <- rd(file.path(IN, "rep_genome_size.tsv")); gs <- gs[!gs$genome_id %in% ORPHANS, ]
    12	gr <- rd(file.path(IN, "rep_growth_v2.tsv"))
    13	gs$logd <- log(gr$d_hours[match(gs$genome_id, gr$rep)])
    14	gs$est_mb <- gs$length_bp / (gs$completeness / 100) / 1e6
    15	cls <- rd(file.path(IN, "atmo_classification_v2.tsv")); cls <- cls[!cls$rep_id %in% ORPHANS, ]
    16	g <- function(x) unique(cls$rep_id[cls$marker == "hydB" & grepl(paste0("Group_", x, "$"), cls$assignment)])
    17	gs$c1l <- gs$genome_id %in% g("1l"); gs$c1h <- gs$genome_id %in% g("1h"); gs$c2a <- gs$genome_id %in% g("2a")
    18	gs$cfI <- gs$genome_id %in% setdiff(readLines(file.path(IN, "guild_coxL_reps_v2.txt")), ORPHANS)
    19	tax <- rbind(rd(file.path(IN, "r232_bac120.summary.tsv"))[, 1:2], rd(file.path(IN, "r232_ar53.summary.tsv"))[, 1:2])
    20	rk <- function(cl, p) ifelse(grepl(paste0(p, "__[^;]"), cl), sub(paste0(".*", p, "__([^;]*).*"), "\\1", cl), "unassigned")
    21	cl <- tax$classification[match(gs$genome_id, tax$user_genome)]
    22	gs$order <- paste(rk(cl, "p"), rk(cl, "o"), sep = ";")
    23	gs$group <- ifelse(gs$c1l & gs$c1h, "1l+1h", ifelse(gs$c1l, "1l_only", ifelse(gs$c1h, "1h_only", "neither")))
    24	desc <- do.call(rbind, lapply(split(gs, gs$group), function(d) data.frame(group = d$group[1], n = nrow(d),
    25	  median_est_Mb = median(d$est_mb), median_gc = median(d$gc), median_doubling_h = median(exp(d$logd), na.rm = TRUE),
    26	  median_completeness = median(d$completeness))))
    27	fit <- function(resp) {
    28	  m <- lmer(as.formula(paste(resp, "~ c1l + c1h + c2a + cfI + (1 | order)")), gs)
    29	  co <- summary(m)$coefficients
    30	  data.frame(response = resp, term = rownames(co)[-1], estimate = co[-1, 1], se = co[-1, 2],
    31	             p_wald = 2 * pnorm(-abs(co[-1, 3])))
    32	}
    33	gs$log_est_mb <- log(gs$est_mb); gs$gc100 <- 100 * gs$gc
    34	mod <- do.call(rbind, lapply(c("log_est_mb", "gc100", "logd"), fit))
    35	mod$q_BH <- p.adjust(mod$p_wald, "BH")
    36	write.table(desc, file.path(OUT, "trait_medians_by_group.tsv"), sep = "\t", quote = FALSE, row.names = FALSE)
    37	write.table(mod, file.path(OUT, "trait_models_within_order.tsv"), sep = "\t", quote = FALSE, row.names = FALSE)
    38	print(desc); print(mod, digits = 3)
     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	# Life on air (P3): held-out benchmark of the read classifier (review round 1, finding R1-20).
     3	#
     4	# How often is a 100-nt read from a hydrogenase or CoxL gene assigned to the right label when its source
     5	# is absent from the competitive database? Two hold-out schemes, five folds each:
     6	#   genome  hold out all marker proteins of 20% of the representatives (relatives stay in the database)
     7	#   clu50   cluster all EQ marker proteins at 50% identity (MMseqs2, coverage 0.5) and hold out 20% of the
     8	#           clusters, so that no database protein shares >= 50% identity with the read's source protein
     9	# For each fold the database is rebuilt without the held-out EQ proteins (HydDB and Cordero references
    10	# and the SCG set stay), 100-nt fragments are tiled every 30 nt over each held-out gene (alternating
    11	# strand), and 50 random non-marker CDS per held-out genome are tiled the same way as negatives. Reads are
    12	# searched exactly as in r2_reads_array.sh (DIAMOND blastx --id 50 --evalue 1e-5 --max-target-seqs 1,
    13	# best hit with alignment >= 25 aa) and the assigned label is tabulated against the true label at the
    14	# 50/60/70% identity floors. Labels are those of marker_labels_v2.tsv (corrected CoxL forms, group 2a).
    15	#
    16	#   sbatch b1_classifier_benchmark.sh      (workbench 06_atmospheric/lifeonair_review_r1)
    17	#
    18	#SBATCH --job-name=loa_bench
    19	#SBATCH --output=/ibex/scratch/projects/c2014/rob/metagenomics/06_atmospheric/lifeonair_review_r1/logs/bench_%j.out
    20	#SBATCH --error=/ibex/scratch/projects/c2014/rob/metagenomics/06_atmospheric/lifeonair_review_r1/logs/bench_%j.err
    21	#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=32
    22	#SBATCH --mem=64G
    23	#SBATCH --time=12:00:00
    24	#SBATCH --partition=batch
    25	
    26	set -euo pipefail
    27	R=/ibex/scratch/projects/c2014/rob/metagenomics
    28	W=$R/06_atmospheric/lifeonair_review_r1
    29	DB=$R/06_atmospheric/lifeonair_reads/db
    30	B=$W/bench
    31	CONDA=/ibex/scratch/projects/c2014/rob/miniconda3/envs
    32	DIAMOND=$CONDA/eggnog/bin/diamond
    33	MMSEQS=$CONDA/mmseqs2/bin/mmseqs
    34	T=${SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK:-32}
    35	source "$CONDA/../bin/activate" bakta   # any env with python3
    36	mkdir -p "$B" "$W/logs" "$W/results"
    37	LAB=$W/inputs/marker_labels_v2.tsv       # copied from lifeonair/results/reads/db/marker_labels_v2.tsv
    38	[ -s "$LAB" ] || { echo "ERROR: $LAB missing"; exit 1; }
    39	
    40	echo "[$(date -Iseconds)] cluster EQ marker proteins at 50% identity"
    41	awk '/^>/{p = ($0 ~ /^>EQ\|/)} p' "$DB/markers.faa" > "$B/eq_markers.faa"
    42	"$MMSEQS" easy-cluster "$B/eq_markers.faa" "$B/clu50" "$B/tmp_clu" --min-seq-id 0.5 -c 0.5 \
    43	    --threads "$T" >/dev/null 2>&1
    44	rm -rf "$B/tmp_clu"
    45	
    46	echo "[$(date -Iseconds)] folds, gene nucleotide sequences and fragments"
    47	python3 - "$R" "$B" "$LAB" <<'PY'
    48	import csv, os, random, sys
    49	R, B, LAB = sys.argv[1:4]
    50	TREE = "/ibex/scratch/projects/c2014/EmptyQuarter_Data/processed/sequencing-results/sites"
    51	meta = {r["sample"]: r for r in csv.DictReader(open(f"{R}/metadata/sample_metadata.tsv"), delimiter="\t")}
    52	
    53	def resolve(rep):                              # same resolver as r1_build_db.sh
    54	    sample = rep.split("__")[0]; m = meta.get(sample)
    55	    if "__" not in rep or not m:
    56	        return None
    57	    tok, local = rep.split("__", 1)[1].split("_", 1)
    58	    anno = "single_sample" if tok == "single" else "self_supervised"
    59	    base = f"{TREE}/{m['trip']}/site{m['site']}/{sample}/wgs/annotation/{anno}/bakta"
    60	    for lc in [local] + ([local[:-4]] if local.endswith("_sub") else []):
    61	        p = f"{base}/{lc}/{lc}.ffn"
    62	        if os.path.exists(p):
    63	            return p
    64	    return None
    65	
    66	def read_fa(path):
    67	    name, seq = None, []
    68	    for line in open(path):
    69	        if line.startswith(">"):
    70	            if name is not None: yield name, "".join(seq)
    71	            name, seq = line[1:].split()[0], []
    72	        else: seq.append(line.strip())
    73	    if name is not None: yield name, "".join(seq)
    74	
    75	label = {r["subject"]: (r["marker"], r["label"]) for r in csv.DictReader(open(LAB), delimiter="\t")
    76	         if r["subject"].startswith("EQ|")}
    77	clu = {m: c for c, m in (l.rstrip("\n").split("\t") for l in open(f"{B}/clu50_cluster.tsv"))}
    78	reps = sorted({s.split("|")[2] for s in label})
    79	rng = random.Random(7)
    80	rfold = {r: i % 5 for i, r in enumerate(rng.sample(reps, len(reps)))}
    81	cl = sorted(set(clu.values()))
    82	cfold = {c: i % 5 for i, c in enumerate(rng.sample(cl, len(cl)))}
    83	with open(f"{B}/folds.tsv", "w") as o:
    84	    o.write("subject\tmarker\tlabel\tcluster50\tfold_genome\tfold_clu50\n")
    85	    for s, (m, l) in sorted(label.items()):
    86	        o.write(f"{s}\t{m}\t{l}\t{clu[s]}\t{rfold[s.split('|')[2]]}\t{cfold[clu[s]]}\n")
    87	
    88	def tile(seq, step=30, L=100):
    89	    rc = str.maketrans("ACGTN", "TGCAN")
    90	    for k, i in enumerate(range(0, max(len(seq) - L, 0) + 1, step)):
    91	        f = seq[i:i + L]
    92	        yield f if k % 2 == 0 else f.translate(rc)[::-1]
    93	
    94	by_rep = {}
    95	for s in label:
    96	    by_rep.setdefault(s.split("|")[2], set()).add(s.split("|")[3])
    97	nmiss = 0
    98	with open(f"{B}/fragments.fa", "w") as o, open(f"{B}/fragments_truth.tsv", "w") as t:
    99	    t.write("read\tsource\tmarker\tlabel\n")
   100	    for rep, pids in sorted(by_rep.items()):
   101	        p = resolve(rep)
   102	        if not p:
   103	            nmiss += 1; continue
   104	        seqs = dict(read_fa(p))
   105	        for pid in sorted(pids):
   106	            subj = [k for k in (f"EQ|hydB|{rep}|{pid}", f"EQ|coxL|{rep}|{pid}") if k in label][0]
   107	            g = seqs.get(pid)
   108	            if not g:
   109	                continue
   110	            for j, f in enumerate(tile(g.upper())):
   111	                rid = f"{subj}#{j}"
   112	                o.write(f">{rid}\n{f}\n"); t.write(f"{rid}\t{subj}\t{label[subj][0]}\t{label[subj][1]}\n")
   113	        others = [k for k in seqs if k not in pids and len(seqs[k]) >= 300]
   114	        for pid in rng.sample(others, min(50, len(others))):
   115	            for j, f in enumerate(tile(seqs[pid].upper(), step=150)):
   116	                rid = f"NEG|{rep}|{pid}#{j}"
   117	                o.write(f">{rid}\n{f}\n"); t.write(f"{rid}\tNEG|{rep}|{pid}\tnone\tnone\n")
   118	print(f"reps {len(reps)}; unresolved {nmiss}; clusters {len(cl)}", flush=True)
   119	PY
   120	
   121	for SCHEME in genome clu50; do
   122	  for F in 0 1 2 3 4; do
   123	    D=$B/${SCHEME}_f$F; mkdir -p "$D"
   124	    [ -s "$D/hits.tsv" ] && continue
   125	    COL=$([ "$SCHEME" = genome ] && echo 5 || echo 6)
   126	    awk -F'\t' -v c=$COL -v f=$F 'NR > 1 && $c == f {print $1}' "$B/folds.tsv" > "$D/heldout.txt"
   127	    awk 'NR == FNR {h[">" $1]; next} /^>/{p = !(($1) in h)} p' "$D/heldout.txt" "$DB/loa_reads.faa" > "$D/db.faa"
   128	    "$DIAMOND" makedb --in "$D/db.faa" -d "$D/db" -p "$T" --quiet
   129	    # reads: fragments of the held-out marker genes plus negatives from the genomes of held-out proteins
   130	    python3 - "$D/heldout.txt" "$B/fragments.fa" "$D/reads.fa" <<'PY'
   131	import sys
   132	held = {l.strip() for l in open(sys.argv[1])}
   133	reps = {h.split("|")[2] for h in held}
   134	keep = False
   135	with open(sys.argv[3], "w") as o:
   136	    for line in open(sys.argv[2]):
   137	        if line.startswith(">"):
   138	            rid = line[1:].strip(); src = rid.split("#")[0]
   139	            keep = src in held or (src.startswith("NEG|") and src.split("|")[1] in reps)
   140	        if keep:
   141	            o.write(line)
   142	PY
   143	    "$DIAMOND" blastx -q "$D/reads.fa" -d "$D/db" -p "$T" --id 50 --evalue 1e-5 --max-target-seqs 1 \
   144	        --outfmt 6 qseqid sseqid pident length evalue bitscore --quiet -o "$D/hits.tsv"
   145	    rm -f "$D/db.faa" "$D/db.dmnd"
   146	    echo "[$(date -Iseconds)] $SCHEME fold $F: $(grep -c '^>' "$D/reads.fa") reads, $(cut -f1 "$D/hits.tsv" | sort -u | wc -l) hit"
   147	  done
   148	done
   149

   152	import csv, sys
   153	from collections import Counter
   154	B, LAB0, LAB, OUT = sys.argv[1:5]
   155	lab = {r["subject"]: r["label"] for r in csv.DictReader(open(LAB0), delimiter="\t")}
   156	lab.update({r["subject"]: r["label"] for r in csv.DictReader(open(LAB), delimiter="\t")})
   157	def group(l):
   158	    for k, g in (("Group_1l", "hydB_1l"), ("Group_1h", "hydB_1h"), ("Group_2a", "hydB_2a")):
   159	        if l.endswith(k): return g
   160	    if l == "form_I": return "coxL_formI"
   161	    if l in ("form_II", "coxL_other", "coxL_unresolved"): return "coxL_other"
   162	    if l in ("none",): return "none"
   163	    if "NiFe" in l or "FeFe" in l or "Fe" in l: return "other_nife"
   164	    return "SCG"
   165	truth = {r["read"]: group(r["label"]) for r in csv.DictReader(open(f"{B}/fragments_truth.tsv"), delimiter="\t")}
   166	rows = []
   167	for scheme in ("genome", "clu50"):
   168	    for floor in (50, 60, 70):
   169	        c = Counter(); n = Counter()
   170	        for f in range(5):
   171	            D = f"{B}/{scheme}_f{f}"
   172	            held = {l.strip() for l in open(f"{D}/heldout.txt")}
   173	            reps = {h.split("|")[2] for h in held}
   174	            best = {}
   175	            for line in open(f"{D}/hits.tsv"):
   176	                q, s, pid, aln = line.split("\t")[:4]
   177	                if q not in best and int(aln) >= 25:
   178	                    best[q] = (s, float(pid))
   179	            for rid, t in truth.items():
   180	                src = rid.split("#")[0]
   181	                if not (src in held or (src.startswith("NEG|") and src.split("|")[1] in reps)):
   182	                    continue
   183	                n[t] += 1
   184	                h = best.get(rid)
   185	                a = group(lab[h[0]]) if h and h[1] >= floor else "unassigned"
   186	                c[(t, a)] += 1
   187	        for (t, a), k in sorted(c.items()):
   188	            rows.append((scheme, floor, t, a, k, n[t], k / n[t]))
   189	with open(f"{OUT}/classifier_benchmark_confusion.tsv", "w") as o:
   190	    o.write("scheme\tid_floor\ttrue\tassigned\treads\treads_true\tfraction\n")
   191	    for r in rows: o.write("\t".join(map(str, r[:-1])) + f"\t{r[-1]:.4f}\n")
   192	# precision / recall per marker group (negatives and SCG counted as possible false positives)
   193	with open(f"{OUT}/classifier_benchmark_summary.tsv", "w") as o:
   194	    o.write("scheme\tid_floor\tgroup\trecall\tprecision\tassigned_reads\n")
   195	    for scheme in ("genome", "clu50"):
   196	        for floor in (50, 60, 70):
   197	            sub = [r for r in rows if r[0] == scheme and r[1] == floor]
   198	            for g in ("hydB_1l", "hydB_1h", "hydB_2a", "other_nife", "coxL_formI", "coxL_other"):
   199	                tp = sum(r[4] for r in sub if r[2] == g and r[3] == g)
   200	                pos = sum(r[4] for r in sub if r[2] == g)
   201	                ass = sum(r[4] for r in sub if r[3] == g)
   202	                o.write(f"{scheme}\t{floor}\t{g}\t{tp / pos if pos else float('nan'):.4f}\t"
   203	                        f"{tp / ass if ass else float('nan'):.4f}\t{ass}\n")
   204	print(open(f"{OUT}/classifier_benchmark_summary.tsv").read())

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Life on air (P3): corrected CoxL form calls (review round 1, finding R1-01; 12 October 2026).
     3	
     4	The original typing (IBEX lifeonair_clean/scripts/lifeonair_2_type_guild.sh) had the active-site motifs
     5	inverted: it called AYRGAGR form I and AYR[CG]SFR form II. Form I CoxL carries AYXCSFR and form II carries
     6	AYRGAGR (King & Weber 2007, Nat Rev Microbiol 5:107); the three form-I references of Cordero et al. 2019
     7	used here carry AYRCSFR, AYACSFR and AYRCSFR. Corrected rule, per protein:
     8	  - AY.CSFR present                       -> form_I
     9	  - AYRGAGR present (and no AY.CSFR)      -> form_II
    10	  - neither motif (motif region missing)  -> form_I if > 55% identity and >= 90% query coverage to the
    11	                                             Cordero form-I references, otherwise unresolved
    12	Reads are relabelled without re-running DIAMOND: every EQ CoxL protein was already a separate subject in
    13	the competitive database, so only the label attached to each subject changes.
    14	
    15	    python3 lifeonair/code/fix_coxL_v2.py <coxL.faa from IBEX lifeonair_clean/queries>
    16	
    17	Writes lifeonair/results/inputs/coxL_class_v2.tsv, lifeonair/results/inputs/guild_coxL_reps_v2.txt and
    18	lifeonair/results/reads/db/marker_labels_v2.tsv (also separates group 2a hydrogenases, R1-02, by keeping
    19	their HydDB label; r3_aggregate.py maps it to its own column).
    20	"""
    21	import csv
    22	import re
    23	import sys
    24	from collections import Counter
    25	
    26	IN, DB = "lifeonair/results/inputs", "lifeonair/results/reads/db"
    27	FORM_I, FORM_II = re.compile(r"AY.CSFR"), re.compile(r"AYRGAGR")
    28	
    29	
    30	def read_fasta(path):
    31	    name, seq = None, []
    32	    for line in open(path):
    33	        if line.startswith(">"):
    34	            if name is not None:
    35	                yield name, "".join(seq)
    36	            name, seq = line[1:].split()[0], []
    37	        else:
    38	            seq.append(line.strip())
    39	    if name is not None:
    40	        yield name, "".join(seq)
    41	
    42	
    43	def main(faa):
    44	    old = {r["query"]: r for r in csv.DictReader(open(f"{IN}/coxL_class.tsv"), delimiter="\t")}
    45	    calls, n = {}, Counter()
    46	    with open(f"{IN}/coxL_class_v2.tsv", "w") as o:
    47	        o.write("query\tlength\tpident_formI\tqcov_formI\tmotif\tform_call\told_call\n")
    48	        for name, seq in read_fasta(faa):
    49	            r = old[name]
    50	            pid, qc = float(r["pident_formI"]), float(r["qcov_formI"])
    51	            if FORM_I.search(seq):
    52	                mot, call = "AYxCSFR", "form_I"
    53	            elif FORM_II.search(seq):
    54	                mot, call = "AYRGAGR", "form_II"
    55	            else:
    56	                mot = "none"
    57	                call = "form_I" if (pid > 55.0 and qc >= 90.0) else "unresolved"
    58	            calls[name] = call
    59	            n[(mot, call, r["form_I_call"])] += 1
    60	            o.write(f"{name}\t{len(seq)}\t{pid:.1f}\t{qc:.1f}\t{mot}\t{call}\t{r['form_I_call']}\n")
    61	    for k, v in sorted(n.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):
    62	        print(*k, v, sep="\t")
    63	    reps = sorted({q.split("|")[0] for q, c in calls.items() if c == "form_I"})
    64	    open(f"{IN}/guild_coxL_reps_v2.txt", "w").write("\n".join(reps) + "\n")
    65	    print(f"form_I proteins {sum(c == 'form_I' for c in calls.values())}; form_I reps {len(reps)}")
    66	    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(f"{DB}/marker_labels.tsv"), delimiter="\t"))
    67	    with open(f"{DB}/marker_labels_v2.tsv", "w", newline="") as f:
    68	        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()), delimiter="\t")
    69	        w.writeheader()
    70	        for r in rows:
    71	            if r["marker"] == "coxL" and r["subject"].startswith("EQ|"):
    72	                q = "|".join(r["subject"].split("|")[2:4])
    73	                r["label"] = {"form_I": "form_I", "form_II": "form_II"}.get(calls[q], "coxL_unresolved")
    74	            w.writerow(r)
    75	
    76	
    77	if __name__ == "__main__":
    78	    main(sys.argv[1])
     1	#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
     2	# Life on air (P3): batch-stratified decontam frequency screen (review round 1, finding R1-23).
     3	# decontam_screen.R pooled all expeditions and extraction kits, so its frequency test compared samples
     4	# whose DNA yield differs by batch. Here the two models (abundance independent of DNA concentration vs
     5	# proportional to 1/concentration) are compared after centring within expedition x extraction kit, with a
     6	# pooled F statistic over strata (df = n - number of strata). Flags at score < 0.1 with >= 10 samples.
     7	#   Rscript lifeonair/code/decontam_batch.R   -> lifeonair/results/decontam/rep_frequency_by_batch.tsv
     8	IN <- "lifeonair/results/inputs"; OUT <- "lifeonair/results/decontam"
     9	rd <- function(f, ...) read.delim(f, quote = "", comment.char = "", check.names = FALSE, ...)
    10	ab <- rd(gzfile(file.path(IN, "coverm_rep_abundance_long.tsv.gz")), header = FALSE, col.names = c("sample", "rep", "abund"))
    11	ab <- ab[ab$rep != "unmapped" & is.finite(ab$abund) & ab$abund > 0, ]
    12	led <- rd(file.path(IN, "sample_ledger_dna.tsv")); led$conc <- suppressWarnings(as.numeric(led$dna_concentration))
    13	led <- led[is.finite(led$conc) & led$conc > 0, ]
    14	nu <- tapply(led$conc, led$sample_id, function(x) length(unique(round(x, 6))))
    15	led <- led[!led$sample_id %in% names(nu)[nu > 1] & !duplicated(led$sample_id), ]
    16	led$batch <- paste(tolower(led$trip), led$dna_kit)
    17	ab <- ab[ab$sample %in% led$sample_id, ]
    18	ab$batch <- led$batch[match(ab$sample, led$sample_id)]; ab$x <- log(led$conc[match(ab$sample, led$sample_id)])
    19	cat("samples", length(unique(ab$sample)), "batches:\n"); print(table(led$batch[led$sample_id %in% ab$sample]))
    20	res <- do.call(rbind, lapply(split(ab, ab$rep), function(d) {
    21	  n <- nrow(d); k <- length(unique(d$batch))
    22	  if (n < 10 || n - k < 5) return(data.frame(rep = d$rep[1], n_present = n, n_batches = k, score = NA))
    23	  y <- log(d$abund); r1 <- y + d$x
    24	  cy <- y - ave(y, d$batch); cr <- r1 - ave(r1, d$batch)
    25	  data.frame(rep = d$rep[1], n_present = n, n_batches = k, score = pf(sum(cr^2) / sum(cy^2), n - k, n - k))
    26	}))
    27	res$flag <- !is.na(res$score) & res$score < 0.1
    28	write.table(res, file.path(OUT, "rep_frequency_by_batch.tsv"), sep = "\t", quote = FALSE, row.names = FALSE)
    29	g <- unique(c(readLines(file.path(IN, "guild_hydB_reps.txt")), readLines(file.path(IN, "guild_coxL_reps_v2.txt"))))
    30	cat(sprintf("testable %d; flagged %d (guild carriers %d)\n", sum(!is.na(res$score)), sum(res$flag), sum(res$flag & res$rep %in% g)))
    31	writeLines(res$rep[res$flag], file.path(OUT, "flagged_reps_by_batch.txt"))

## Pipeline (only step 2 is new compute)
1. **Census** — count reps with hydB/coxL KO in rep_ko.tsv. DONE (below).
2. **HydDB typing** (NEW, small) — extract hydB(K06281)/coxL(K03520) proteins of the guild reps from reps.fa /
   the rep proteomes, DIAMOND vs HydDB refs + Cordero form-I refs, mafft+FastTree → keep only high-affinity
   (Group 1l/1h) and form-I. Adapt 82b/82c.
3. **Guild** = reps with high-affinity hydB and/or form-I coxL (per-species, non-redundant).
4. **Abundance** — (a) MAG-direct: join guild reps to profiles_full_recompute abund.tsv over the **551 canonical**
   samples, sum guild abundance / total (decide denominator: whole community incl. unmapped ~64%, vs mapped-only),
   median. (b) 16S: recompute guild prevalence-per-genus on 5,438 × companion-16S abundance (old method = 13.5%).
   **Report BOTH** (they bracket; reviewer faulted 16S-alone).

# lifeonair_reads: read-based atmospheric trace-gas guild capacity (P3, life on air)

Produced 9-10 October 2026 for the Empty Quarter P3 manuscript (repository
`bio-ontology-research-group/empty-quarter-mags`, code in `code/lifeonair/`, results in `results/lifeonair/`).

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| `db/` | Competitive DIAMOND database: EQ hydB (K06281) proteins of the 5,438 ANI95 reps labelled by HydDB class, all HydDB references, EQ coxL labelled form I / other (Cordero identity + AYRGAGR), Cordero form-I references, 14 single-copy ribosomal proteins (90% clustered). `db.sha256`. Job 53522301. |
| `per_sample_L100/<sample>/` | Per sample: 10 M R1 reads (seqtk seed 11) truncated to 100 nt, DIAMOND 2.0.15 blastx best hit, alignment >= 25 aa; `subject_counts.tsv` = reads per subject at >= 50/60/70% identity; `meta.tsv`. Array 53524441 (573 samples). |
| `per_sample/` | SUPERSEDED first array 53522544 (alignment >= 40 aa excluded the 101-nt trip-4 libraries). Do not use. |
| `per_sample_counts.tar.gz` | Tarball of `per_sample_L100` as mirrored into the repository (`results/lifeonair/reads/per_sample/`). |
| `operon/` | +/-10-gene Bakta/eggNOG context of every typed hydB/coxL. Job 53524544. |
| `trees/` | mafft --auto + FastTree -lg -gamma trees: hydB (EQ 70% clusters + HydDB NiFe groups 1-2), coxL (EQ >= 600 aa 70% clusters + Cordero refs). Job 53535825 (53524702 failed: selenocysteine U in refs). |
| `coverm_rep_abundance_long.tsv.gz` | Long table of CoverM relative abundance per sample x rep from `rebuild_20260729/picrust2_current_20260803/coverm_current/profiles_full_recompute`. |

Genome equivalents are relative capacity (copies per single-copy-gene genome equivalent), not cell
fractions or activity. Group 1l genomes carry ~1.9 copies on average.

 succeeded in 0ms:
96\% and 100\%; the medium and small CoxL subunits co-occurred with 96\% of form-I CoxL. In bulk soil, the
median sample carried 1.08 group 1l copies per genome equivalent, 0.14 group 1h, 0.03 group 2a and 0.11
form-I CoxL. The read classifier was specific for these groups when the source genes were withheld. With
all proteins sharing 50\% identity with the source removed from the database, 89\% of group 1l fragments
were assigned to group 1l and 0.7\% to group 1h, 99\% of group 1h fragments to group 1h, 94\% of group 2a
fragments to group 2a and 82\% of form-I CoxL fragments to form I; of 1.5 million fragments from
non-marker genes, two were assigned to group 1l (Table~\ref{tab:benchmark}).

\subsection*{Root-adjacent soil carries less group 1l relative to other genes}
We compared root-adjacent and bulk soil in the 106 site visits at 47 sites where both were sampled at
5--10~cm. Group 1l GE were lower beside roots by a median of 2.27 log$_2$ units, a 4.8-fold difference (95\%
CI $-2.90$ to $-1.59$; lower in 89\% of visits; $p=3\times10^{-7}$; Fig.~\ref{fig:guilds}b). The control
hydrogenases and CoxL were lower by 0.40 and 0.28 units, and the excess change of group 1l beyond the
controls was $-1.83$ ($-2.62$ to $-1.00$; $q=2\times10^{-6}$). Groups 1h ($-0.01$), 2a ($-0.02$) and form-I
CoxL ($-0.05$) changed no more than the controls (all $q>0.8$; Fig.~\ref{fig:guilds}d). In the mixed model,
marker and position interacted ($\chi^2=324$, 5 df), and only the group 1l interaction departed from the
reference ($-1.75$, SE 0.12; other high-affinity groups between $-0.08$ and $+0.07$). Position accounted for
a gain in marginal $R^2$ of 0.22 for group 1l and 0.02--0.10 for the other markers.

The difference was consistent across expeditions, analysis choices and lineages. The excess change of group
1l was negative in each expedition ($-0.99$ to $-2.63$), between $-1.54$ and $-1.85$ in every sensitivity
variant (Table~\ref{tab:sensitivity}), and $-1.82$ without a pseudocount and $-1.32$ with a pseudocount of
0.1. All ten orders of group 1l carriers with the highest share of bulk soil had a lower share beside roots
(Table~\ref{tab:lineages}).

\subsection*{The depletion of group 1l scales with DNA yield and with genus turnover}
Copies per genome equivalent fall either when carriers decline or when other organisms increase. Extract
DNA concentration, a proxy that combines microbial biomass with extraction efficiency and plant DNA, was a
median 10-fold higher in root-adjacent than in bulk soil (log$_2$ ratio 3.37, 47 visits). Across 267
samples, group 1l GE fell by 0.32 log$_2$ units (SE 0.03) per doubling of DNA concentration, within visits
($-0.30$) as well as between them ($-0.35$), within visits extracted with a single kit ($-0.36$) and within
bulk soil alone ($-0.23$), whereas groups 1h and 2a ($-0.02$ and $-0.03$) and the controls did not scale with
DNA (Fig.~\ref{fig:scaling}a). Under the scaling model this slope corresponds to an exponent of about 0.7:
if DNA concentration tracked biomass, group 1l copies per gram would increase less than proportionally
with biomass. Adjusting for DNA concentration reduced the root effect on group 1l from $-2.12$ (SE 0.23) to
$-1.36$ (SE 0.20; Fig.~\ref{fig:scaling}c). Because DNA concentration is not a calibrated measure of
microbial biomass, these data establish a relative depletion of group 1l and a sub-proportional scaling
with DNA yield, not a loss of group 1l carriers per gram of soil.

The change was carried by lineage composition. The share of the mapped community held by group 1l
carriers fell from a median of 0.31 in bulk soil to 0.06 beside roots, and genus turnover
accounted for 97\% of this change; the carrier fraction within genera changed little
($-0.005$). The share of carriers fell by 2.17 log$_2$ units, their copy-weighted share by 2.30 and
the number of copies per carrier by only 0.20, so the depletion reflects fewer carrier genomes rather than
fewer copies in each.

\subsection*{Genera carrying group 1l are depleted beyond their predicted growth potential}
Root-adjacent communities had shorter predicted doubling times than bulk soil in 79\% of 106 visits
(community-weighted log doubling time $-0.26$; $p=1.4\times10^{-5}$), consistent with the enrichment of
fast-growing taxa in rhizospheres \citep{lopez2023rhizosphere}. Within orders, carriers of group 1l
($+0.12$, SE 0.03), group 1h ($+0.12$) and group 2a ($+0.20$) had longer predicted

codex
The main paired root contrast reproduces in the saved outputs. I found a substantive mismatch in the spatial claim: the manuscript says the surface pattern survives every precipitation product, but the NASA POWER and blended-product results do not support that statement. I’m also checking joins and cohort construction, because some spatial outputs report 60 root sites despite a 59-site primary cohort.

Search prompts: `search/round1/prompts/`; raw outputs with Flash syntheses: `search/round1/out/`.
